Ablations show that the hierarchical error-cause memory and double-confidence filtering both contribute to these gains.

</details>


### [315] [When Features Become Instances: Inverted Contrastive Learning for Unsupervised Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00782)
*Utsab Ghosh,Roshni Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ICLFS，一种基于倒置对比学习的无监督特征选择方法。通过对数据矩阵进行转置使每个特征由其样本轮廓向量表示，构建多个掩码正视图和打乱负视图，在InfoNCE目标下学习投影空间的稳定表示，并利用投影空间嵌入范数作为特征重要性排序信号，辅以拉普拉斯门控排序校正来抑制局部冗余特征。在12个基准数据集上，ICLFS在10个数据集上取得了最优聚类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督特征选择方法依赖间接的结构性准则（如相似性保持、局部性、稀疏性、簇几何或重构质量），难以在没有类标签的情况下有效定义特征效用。作者希望从表征一致性的角度重新审视无监督特征选择问题，找到一种更强、更有效的特征排序标准。

Method: ICLFS首先将数据矩阵转置，使每个特征以其样本轮廓向量表示，从而将问题转化为特征层面的表征学习。接着构建多个掩码正视图和随机打乱负视图，在InfoNCE目标下学习投影空间中的一致性表示。受余弦和InfoNCE训练对嵌入范数影响的启发，利用投影空间嵌入范数作为特征显著性排序信号，并通过拉普拉斯门控排序校正来消除局部冗余候选特征，同时保留关键特征。

Result: 在12个基准数据集上，按照标准的基于聚类的无监督特征选择评估协议，ICLFS在10个数据集上取得了最佳聚类准确率，优于经典方法和神经基线方法，在其余2个数据集上也保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 特征层面的对比表征一致性为基于邻域、簇结构及重构的无监督特征选择方法提供了一个强而有效的替代方案，验证了从表征一致性角度研究无监督特征选择的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Unsupervised feature selection seeks a compact subset of informative features without access to class labels, making feature utility difficult to define. Existing UFS methods therefore rely on indirect structural criteria, such as similarity preservation, locality, sparsity, cluster geometry, or reconstruction quality. In this paper, we instead study UFS through representation consistency and propose Inverted Contrastive Learning for Unsupervised Feature Selection (ICLFS), a feature-wise contrastive framework that reformulates UFS as a representation learning problem over features rather than samples. ICLFS first inverts the data matrix so that each feature is represented by its sample-profile vector, then constructs multiple masked positive views together with a shuffled negative view, and learns projector-space representations that remain consistent across these structured perturbations under an InfoNCE-based objective. Motivated by recent findings that cosine-based and InfoNCE-based training affect embedding norms, we use projector-space embedding magnitude as the saliency signal for ranking features. The resulting norm-based ranking is subsequently refined through Laplacian-Gated Ranking Correction, which suppresses locally redundant candidates while preserving salient ones. Extensive experiments on 12 benchmark datasets show that ICLFS achieves the best clustering accuracy on 10 datasets against both classical and neural baselines under the standard clustering-based UFS evaluation protocol, while remaining competitive on the other two. These results show that feature-wise contrastive representation consistency provides a strong and effective alternative to neighborhood, cluster, and reconstruction-based UFS formulations.

</details>


### [316] [StudyBench: Can Self-Evolution Squeeze Textbooks for Olympiad Capability?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00787)
*Yinghao Chen,Zixi Chen,Bingxiang He,Ziqing Qiao,Huan-ang Gao,Yinuo Xu,Yuxin Zuo,Zeyuan Liu,Yuhao Zhan,Chaojun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出StudyBench，一个受控的物理基准，用于直接衡量自我进化方法将训练材料转化为能力的效率。基准分为应用集和迁移集，分别评估吸收能力和迁移能力。实验发现：改进在应用集上很少转化为迁移集上的提升，存在"指导差距"（Guidance Gap）和"计算平台期"（Compute Plateau），表明剩余差距是方法问题而非数据或计算问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理想的自我进化方法应能像人类一样，仅从少量优质教科书材料中自主学习并获得可迁移的问题解决能力，但目前缺乏直接衡量这一能力的指标。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供一个可控的基准来量化自我进化方法的效率。

Method: 构建StudyBench基准，包含应用集（难度较高的教科书问题，评估吸收能力）和迁移集（奥林匹克竞赛级问题，评估迁移能力）；在三个基础模型上对代表性自我进化方法进行基准测试，并设计指导消融实验来区分方法差距与数据/计算差距。

Result: 发现三个关键现象：1) 应用集上的改进很少迁移到更难的迁移集；2) 存在指导差距，即使在最强的方法下，同种材料作为情境指导解锁的能力远超方法内化所达成的；3) 所有方法都遇到计算平台期，在计算预算耗尽前就已饱和。

Conclusion: 剩余的性能差距本质上是方法问题而非数据或计算资源问题。StudyBench将自我进化的进展从开放式的追求转变为可衡量的研究目标，提供了该领域的受控测试平台。

Abstract: Humans need to study only a handful of well-written textbooks to master a discipline and attempt its hardest problems. We argue that an ideal self-evolution method should share the same property, that is autonomously learning from raw training material for transferable problem-solving capability. However, we still lack a direct measurement for it. We introduce StudyBench, a controlled physics benchmark that directly measures how efficiently a self-evolution method converts training material into capability. We organise the test set into an Application Set, consisting of difficult textbook problems and evaluating absorption ability, and a Transfer Set, consisting of olympiad-level problems and evaluating transfer ability. Benchmarking representative self-evolution methods across three base models, we find that improvements on the Application Set rarely translate to the harder Transfer Set. A guidance ablation exposes a Guidance Gap: even the strongest method closes only a small fraction of what the same material unlocks when supplied as in-context guidance. Besides, every method hits a Compute Plateau, saturating well before exhausting its compute budget. The remaining gap is therefore a method problem rather than a data or compute problem. By offering a clean and controlled benchmark, StudyBench turns self-evolution progress from an open-ended pursuit into a measurable target for future research. Our code is released at https://github.com/thunlp/StudyBench.

</details>


### [317] [Towards a Reliable and Practical Eval Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00805)
*Emma Thuong Nguyen,Abhishek Ghose*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端的评估（eval）流水线，将评估清单创建与学习聚合相结合，以提高LLM评判器之间的一致性以及与人类判断的准确率，并提供自我一致性、解释和预测不确定性等功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的软件系统越来越需要有效的评估作为开发生命周期中的质量门禁，但现有工作通常只解决评估可靠性的个别方面，而缺乏一套完整的实践需求解决方案。

Method: 提出一个端到端的评估流水线，包含两个核心部分：(1) 评估清单（checklist）创建；(2) 针对清单响应的学习聚合方法，用于提升多个LLM评判器之间的一致性以及与人类判断的匹配度。

Result: 该框架还提供自我一致性（self-consistency）、解释（explanations）和预测不确定性（prediction uncertainty）等能力，并通过实证实验证明了其有效性。

Conclusion: 该端到端评估框架系统性地解决了LLM评估可靠性中的多个实际问题，为LLM软件系统的质量保障提供了更全面的解决方案。

Abstract: LLM-based software systems increasingly require effective "evals" as quality gates in the development lifecycle. However, existing work typically addresses individual aspects of eval reliability rather than the full set of practical requirements. We present an end-to-end eval pipeline that combines eval checklist creation, with learned aggregation for checklist responses, to improve agreement across LLM judges and accuracy against human judgments. The framework additionally pro- vides self-consistency, explanations, and prediction uncertainty, and we empirically demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [318] [One Policy, Any Budget: Internalizing Budget-Aware Search via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00813)
*Xiaowei Sun,Jin Li,Yili Hong,Yikun Fu,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AnySearch是一个框架，通过训练脚手架和课程强化学习，使单个基于LLM的搜索智能体能够在任意预算约束下进行预算感知的搜索，在七大QA基准上超越了基线方法，并泛化到训练范围之外的未见约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于强化学习的LLM搜索智能体在固定预算下训练，当部署时约束条件变化时无法适应，缺乏跨预算约束的灵活性。

Method: 两阶段训练：第一阶段使用显式预算状态注入和结构化推理提示，在线性递减预算下训练；第二阶段移除脚手架，让智能体在自适应采样的预算约束下自主操作以匹配推理条件。采用组合奖励函数，通过绝对和相对信号耦合答案准确性与预算效率，并用自适应权重对高准确率查询放大效率信号、对低准确率查询衰减效率信号。

Result: 在七个通用和多跳QA基准上的大量实验表明，该方法在所有预算规模下均优于基线，能泛化到训练范围之外的未见约束，并在不产生过多token开销的情况下实现了更高的工具生产力。

Conclusion: AnySearch通过预算感知的训练框架实现了单一策略在任意预算约束下的自适应搜索，为LLM搜索智能体的预算灵活性提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: While reinforcement learning has enabled LLM-based search agents to invoke external tools, existing methods train under fixed budgets and cannot adapt when constraints vary at deployment. We propose AnySearch, a framework that enables a single policy to perform budget-aware search under any budget constraint through a training scaffold and curriculum reinforcement learning. In the first phase, we train the agent with explicit budget state injection and structured reasoning prompts that guide efficient allocation under linearly decaying budgets. In the second phase, the scaffold is removed and the agent learns to operate autonomously under adaptively sampled budget constraints, matching inference conditions. Both phases are optimized with a composite reward that couples answer accuracy with budget efficiency through absolute and relative signals, where an adaptive weight amplifies the efficiency signal for high-accuracy queries and attenuates it for low-accuracy ones. Extensive experiments on seven general and multi-hop QA benchmarks show that our method outperforms baselines across all budget scales, generalizes to unseen constraints beyond the training range, and achieves superior tool productivity without excessive token overhead. Our code is available at https://github.com/xwsun01/AnySearch.

</details>


### [319] [AnalysisBank: An Expert Analysis Pattern Library for Financial Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00818)
*Yajing Yang,Yunshan Ma,Kelvin J. L. Koa,Min-Yen Kan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AnalysisBank框架，主张财务报告生成应基于"分析层面"（数据信号+分析动作+专家文本片段）而非结构层面（章节/主题）。通过从550份专家报告中提炼出包含47-52种信号类型和13种动作类型的可复用分析库，在推理时匹配数据信号并应用检索到的分析动作来生成报告。在两个财务基准和四种LLM骨干上，相比结构级基线，数据支撑的新颖见解比例提升1.7-3.7倍，且该区分可泛化到科学写作场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有财务报告生成方法大多停留在结构层面，即在预定义的章节框架下填充内容，缺乏数据驱动的深层分析和洞察。作者认为报告生成应在分析层面运作——将内容组织为从数据中推导出的见解，而非依赖高层级的主题或章节结构，以提升报告的新颖性和数据支撑度。

Method: 提出AnalysisBank框架：(1) 从专家报告中提炼"Analyses"组成可复用库，每个Analysis包含数据信号、分析动作及其来源的专家文本片段；(2) 推理时，将输入数据信号与库中条目匹配，检索对应的分析动作来组装报告；(3) 从550份专家报告中蒸馏出分析库，揭示了47-52种信号类型和13种动作类型的重尾分布。

Result: 在两个财务基准数据集上的四种LLM骨干实验中，AnalysisBank相比结构级基线，将新颖、数据支撑的见解比例提升了1.7-3.7倍。此外，将该方法迁移到科学写作领域，验证了分析层面与结构层面的区分具有跨领域的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 财务报告生成应从结构层面转向分析层面，AnalysisBank通过构建可复用的分析库（信号-动作-文本三元组）实现了这一理念，显著提升了报告内容的创新性和数据支撑度，且该区分不仅适用于金融领域，也能推广至更广泛的专业写作场景。

Abstract: We argue that financial report generation should operate at the analytical rather than structural level, composing content from data-derived insights rather than high-level topics or sections. To this end, we propose AnalysisBank, which distills expert reports into a reusable library of Analyses, each pairing a data signal, an analytical move, and the expert span it was derived from. At inference time, AnalysisBank matches input signals to library entries and applies the retrieved moves to compose the report. A study of Analyses distilled from 550 expert reports reveals a heavy-tailed distribution of 47-52 signal types spanning 13 move types. On two financial benchmarks across four LLM backbones, AnalysisBank increases the proportion of novel, data-grounded insights by 1.7-3.7x over structural-level baselines. Transfer to scientific writing suggests that the distinction generalizes beyond finance. Code and the distilled Analysis library are available at https://github.com/yajingyang/AnalysisBank.

</details>


### [320] [Polished but Unresolved: Identifying Late-Stage Pressure States in Long-Horizon Tool-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00823)
*Haoyang Chen,Yi Liu,Jianzhi Shao,Xiaozhou Xu,Zhe Sun,Wei Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了长时程工具使用智能体在"后期压力状态"下的行为——即智能体倾向于在关键约束未解决时过早提交看似完整的最终答案。作者训练线性探针识别该压力状态，并通过激活干预沿压力方向改变智能体行为，进而提出PSPR插件方法缓解压力，在多个基准上增强了现有智能体方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程工具使用智能体不仅需要搜索和规划，还需要决定何时终止任务。然而，智能体在后期可能处于一种"压力状态"，倾向于过早提交看似完整但关键约束未解决的答案，这会导致任务失败。识别和干预这种状态对提升智能体可靠性至关重要。

Method: 1）训练线性探针从隐藏状态中识别压力状态；2）在该压力方向上进行激活干预，观察智能体行为变化；3）通过受控上下文操作验证约束清晰度和动作映射对压力的缓解作用；4）基于发现提出PSPR插件——在中度压力下施加轻量压力缓解方向干预，在高压力风险下转向结构化组织。

Result: 压力状态可从智能体隐藏状态中识别；沿压力方向的激活干预能同时改变压力分数和智能体继续工具使用或提前提交的行为；约束清晰度和动作映射能缓解压力；PSPR方法在多个长时程基准上持续增强了现有智能体方法的性能。

Conclusion: 智能体的"压力状态"是可识别且可干预的，PSPR作为一种轻量级插件方法，通过分层次的干预策略有效缓解后期压力导致的过早提交问题，为长时程工具使用智能体的可靠性提升提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon tool-use agents need not only to search and plan, but also to decide when to finalize. We study late-stage pressure states, in which an agent is biased toward submitting a final answer that appears complete and polished while key constraints remain unresolved. We first train a linear probe to show that this pressure state is identifiable from the agent's hidden states. Then, we use activation interventions along this pressure direction and find that shifting the hidden states changes both the pressure score and whether the agent continues tool use or submits early. Through controlled context manipulations, we further see that the pressure is mitigated by constraint clarity and action mapping. Based on these findings, we propose Probe-Sensed Pressure Relief (PSPR), a plugin that applies lightweight pressure relief direction under moderate pressure and moves to structured organization under high pressure risk. Experiments on multiple long-horizon benchmarks show that our method consistently strengthens existing agent methods.

</details>


### [321] [FLaG: Frequency-Domain Latent-attention Gated Pooling for Token Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00831)
*Kewei Li,Rongying Zhang,Xueli Wang,Xiwen Gong,Zhongjian Wang,Qiuchen Zhao,Lan Huang,Ruochi Zhang,Fengfeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FLaG（频域潜在注意力门控池化），一种即插即用的聚合模块，将编码器输出转换到傅里叶域后再进行池化。通过在AMP活性预测、CIFAR图像分类和多个语言任务上验证，FLaG在多个指标上取得最佳效果，提供了跨蛋白质、视觉和文本表征的可迁移频域聚合偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有token池化方法大多仅在原始token空间操作，未充分利用频域信息来聚合token级表示为固定维度的样本级表示，缺乏一种能在频域中实现更优特征聚合的通用模块。

Method: FLaG通过非冗余rFFT频谱的实部和虚部表示频谱token，利用可学习潜在查询对频谱token进行汇总，推导样本条件通道门控，并重建调制后的token表征用于下游聚合。FLaG作为即插即用模块可嵌入不同的encoder架构。

Result: FLaG在ESM2基AMP活性预测上取得最佳Spearman相关系数、RMSE和Recall@50，在CIFAR-10取得最高top-1准确率，在七个语言指标中的五个取得最佳均值结果（STSBenchmark除外）。机理分析揭示低频预测敏感性、最终层高频敏感性增强及肽特异性位置响应。

Conclusion: FLaG提供了一种跨蛋白质、视觉和文本表征的可迁移频域聚合偏置，其收益取决于骨干网络和下游任务，整体上是一种有效的通用池化模块方案。

Abstract: Token aggregation converts token-level representations into fixed-dimensional sample representations, but most pooling methods operate only in the original token space. We introduce Frequency-Domain Latent-attention Gated Pooling (FLaG), a plug-in aggregation module that re-expresses encoder outputs in the Fourier domain before final pooling. FLaG represents the nonredundant rFFT spectrum through concatenated real and imaginary components, summarizes spectral tokens with learnable latent queries, derives a sample-conditioned channel gate, and reconstructs modulated token representations for downstream aggregation. We evaluate the same architecture across ESM2-based antimicrobial peptide (AMP) activity prediction, ResNet18 image classification on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, and three RoBERTa-based language tasks. FLaG achieves the best macro-averaged Spearman correlation coefficient, RMSE, and Recall@50 across four AMP backbone-species settings and the highest top-1 accuracy on CIFAR 10. It also achieves the best mean results on five of seven language metrics, although mean pooling remains strongest on STSBenchmark. AMP-side mechanistic analyses reveal low-frequency prediction sensitivity across most encoder layers, with increased relative high-frequency sensitivity in the final layer, and pronounced peptide-specific positional responses. The residual gate broadly amplifies spectral channels while preserving the low-frequency-dominated energy profile, whereas latent cross-attention exhibits sample- and species-specific spectral allocation. Overall, FLaG provides a transferable frequency-domain aggregation bias across protein, visual, and textual representations, with benefits that depend on the backbone and downstream task. Supplementary materials, source code, and data are available at https://www.healthinformaticslab.org/supp/ and https://github.com/Kewei2023/AMPCliff/tree/FLaG.

</details>


### [322] [Towards Generalizable Visually Grounded Exploration of Household Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00845)
*Linhao Zheng,Zeming Liu,Wangke Chen,Li Zeng,Wanxiang Che,Heyan Huang,Yuhang Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VGEBench基准，用于评估视觉语言模型在具身探索中的可泛化视觉接地探索能力，通过逻辑驱动的状态机框架模拟多轮交互循环，实验表明现有VLM在此任务上面临重大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身探索范式过度依赖人类标注轨迹的模仿学习，限制了智能体的泛化能力。关键瓶颈在于可泛化的视觉接地探索能力，但现有基准依赖显式文档和标注轨迹，忽视了设备操作中动态的"假设-交互-修正"过程，无法评估该能力。

Method: 构建VGEBench基准，采用逻辑驱动状态机框架模拟多轮交互循环，迫使智能体通过主动视觉感知和反馈驱动修正来实现目标，以此评估VLM的可泛化视觉接地探索能力。

Result: 实验结果表明，现有VLM在将语义知识转化为物理执行以及维持长时程状态追踪方面面临显著挑战。

Conclusion: VGEBench揭示了当前VLM在动态交互式设备操作中的核心短板，为评估和推动通用自主具身智能体的发展提供了重要基准。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in static visual recognition and high-level semantic reasoning. However, current embodied exploration paradigms still heavily rely on imitation learning from human-annotated trajectories, which severely limits agents' generalization ability. The key bottleneck of realizing general autonomous embodied agents lies in Generalizable Visually Grounded Exploration: the ability to operate novel devices without manuals or specific training by actively grounding abstract world knowledge into fine-grained visual affordances. Yet, existing benchmarks fail to evaluate this capability: they generally rely on explicit documents and annotated trajectories, neglecting the dynamic Hypothesis-Interaction-Refinement process essential for functional device operation. To bridge this gap, we introduce VGEBench, a comprehensive benchmark designed to evaluate the generalizable visually grounded exploration capabilities of VLMs. Unlike static datasets, we construct a Logic-Driven State Machine framework. This framework simulates multi-turn interaction loops, compelling agents to achieve goals by active visual perception and feedback-driven correction. Experimental results demonstrate that existing VLMs face significant challenges in translating semantic knowledge into physical execution and maintaining long-horizon state tracking.

</details>


### [323] [Verifiable Disaster Storylines and Causal Knowledge Graphs: A Citation-Grounded Pipeline from Heterogeneous Humanitarian Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00858)
*Ivan Decostanzi,Michele Ronco,Sergio Consoli,Christina Corbane,Lorenzo Bertolini,Indaco Biazzo,Daria Mihaila,Manuel Garcia-Herranz,Felix Schwebel,Yelena Mejova,Kyriaki Kalimeri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将结构化灾难记录（EM-DAT）与非结构化文档（ReliefWeb和欧洲媒体监测）相结合的pipeline，利用检索增强生成（RAG）提取结构化的灾难故事线（覆盖17个字段）并构建带引文解释的因果知识图谱。通过9名领域专家和9名非专家的人工评估，验证了高检索精度、强因果关系的忠实性，以及专家对引文接地组件的明确偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人道主义响应需要快速综合大量异构、高容量的信息来源，这在危机发生后的关键最初几小时内往往超出人类的分析能力，亟需自动化工具辅助态势感知。

Method: 构建一个pipeline，整合EM-DAT结构化灾害记录与ReliefWeb和EMM的非结构化文档；利用检索增强生成（RAG）提取17字段的结构化故事线，并构建因果知识图谱，其中每个节点和边都有引文接地的解释性叙述，支持全链路溯源。

Result: 在三个多样化的危机用例上进行人工评估（9名领域专家和9名非专家）。结果表明检索精度高、提取的因果关系忠实性强，且专家明确偏好引文接地的组件而非无接地替代方案。

Conclusion: 该pipeline设计可扩展至完整EM-DAT目录，目标是公开发布叙事增强版本的数据库，为灾难响应人员和分析师提供可溯源、接地的事实支持。

Abstract: Effective humanitarian response depends on the rapid synthesis of heterogeneous, high-volume information sources - a task that routinely exceeds human analytical capacity in the critical early hours of a crisis. We present a pipeline that combines structured disaster records from EM-DAT with unstructured documents from ReliefWeb and the European Media Monitor (EMM) to produce source-grounded disaster storylines and causal knowledge graphs supporting situational awareness for responders and analysts. Using Retrieval-Augmented Generation, the pipeline extracts structured storylines - tabular event profiles covering 17 fields, from severity and key drivers to child-sensitive impact indicators - and constructs causal knowledge graphs where each node and edge is enriched with citation-grounded explanatory narratives, enabling full traceability back to primary sources. We evaluate the system on three diverse crisis use cases through a human evaluation involving 9 domain expert and 9 non-expert evaluators. Results confirm high retrieval precision, strong faithfulness of extracted causal relations, and a clear expert preference for citation-grounded components over ungrounded alternatives. The pipeline is designed to scale to the full EM-DAT catalogue, with the goal of publicly releasing a narrative-enriched version of the database.

</details>


### [324] [Reinforcement Learning Enhanced LLM Agents for Complex Vehicle Routing Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00859)
*Yi Chen,Zikang Yu,Jiahai Wang,Jinbiao Chen,Jianpeng Zhou,Zizhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RLEA，一种结合强化学习与LLM智能体的多智能体框架，用于自动化建模复杂的车辆路径问题（VRP），在48种VRP变体上相比此前最优方法成功率提升16.67%，同时显著减少运行时错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂VRP变体建模需要大量领域专业知识，限制了先进优化技术的可及性，需要自动化建模的方法来降低这一门槛。

Method: RLEA采用多智能体框架，包含一个通过Soft Q-learning训练的轻量级神经规划器来高效协调LLM智能体的动作；配备进化记忆模块和检索增强生成，使智能体在程序生成和优化过程中利用累积经验及外部求解器知识。

Result: 在48种VRP变体及多种求解器上的实验表明，RLEA相比此前最先进方法成功率提升16.67%，并显著降低运行时错误。

Conclusion: 将强化学习与LLM推理相结合对于自动化优化建模高度有效。

Abstract: Vehicle Routing Problems (VRPs) are fundamental combinatorial optimization problems with widespread applications in various scenarios. The advanced optimization solvers can effectively solve such problems. However, modeling complex VRP variants for solvers often requires substantial domain expertise, which limits the accessibility of advanced optimization technologies. In this paper, we propose Reinforcement Learning Enhanced LLMAgents(RLEA), a multi-agent framework designed to automate the modeling of complex VRPs. RLEA introduces a lightweight neural Planner trained with Soft Q-learning to efficiently orchestrate the actions of LLM-based agents. In addition, we equip the system with an evolutionary memory module and retrieval-augmented generation, enabling the agent to leverage both accumulated experience and external solver knowledge during program generation and refinement for solving VRPs. We evaluated 48 distinct VRP variants across various solvers. The experimental results demonstrate that RLEA outperforms the previous state-of-the-ar method, achieving a 16.67% higher success rate while significantly reducing runtime errors. These results validate that integrating reinforcement learning with LLM-based reasoning is highly effective for automated optimization modeling. The appendix is available at: https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.19134435.

</details>


### [325] [Beyond the Clock: Measuring the Value of Adaptive Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00874)
*Ayushi Chadha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究层级潜在推理器中元层控制（meta-level control）问题，即在任务执行之上，高层控制器何时应保留或修改低层策略承诺。研究发现：在三个预训练种子下，学习到的修订时机产生不同策略（从近乎确定性的早期时钟到高度状态条件化的调度分布），但没有一个能超越固定时机策略在相同冻结检查点上的表现。深入干预研究表明时机本身是有影响的且对顺序敏感，穷举枚举显示强固定调度足以捕获大部分可测量的时机价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体系统变得复合化，越来越多的关键决策超越了任务执行层面本身——高层控制器何时应保留还是修订引导另一过程的策略？作者希望将这一元层控制问题从任务层决策中分离出来进行系统性研究。

Method: 在层级潜在推理器中研究管理器的保留/替换承诺机制，通过三个预训练种子的实验比较学习到的修订时机与强制时机策略；对原始检查点进行深层干预研究，包括顺序敏感性分析和穷举枚举；使用反事实PERSIST/REPLAN诊断分析得分级证据的可信度。

Result: 学习到的修订时机产生定性不同的策略（如近乎确定性的早期时钟 vs 更状态条件化的调度分布），但均不优于最优强制时机策略。固定调度捕获了大部分可测量的时机价值，得分级证据可能因预测由决策位置而非位置内判别性主导而产生误导。

Conclusion: 元层控制评估应从三个独立轴进行：得分是否依赖状态、状态依赖是否改变实际行为、这些改变是否捕获超越强非适应性策略的结果价值。这三者相互独立，核心结论是给定的固定策略可能不必是最佳策略也能接近现有决策预算下的上限。

Abstract: As agentic systems become compound systems, increasingly important decisions move above task execution itself: when should a higher-level controller preserve the strategy guiding another process, and when should it revise it? We study this meta-level control problem in a hierarchical latent reasoner whose manager can retain or replace a commitment governing lower-level computation. Across three precommitted training seeds, learned revision timing produces qualitatively different policies, ranging from an almost deterministic early clock to substantially more state conditioned schedule distributions, yet none outperforms the best forced timing policy evaluated on the same frozen checkpoint. This separates state dependence from decision value: a controller can vary its actions with internal state without turning that variation into a reproducible task-performance benefit. A deeper intervention study on the original checkpoint shows that timing itself is consequential and order-sensitive, while exhaustive enumeration reveals that a strong fixed schedule captures most of the measurable value available from timing at this decision budget. Counterfactual PERSIST/REPLAN diagnostics further show why score-level evidence can be misleading when predictability is dominated by decision position rather than within-position discrimination. Together, these results argue that learned meta-level control should be evaluated along three separate axes: whether its score depends on state, whether that dependence changes realized behavior, and whether those changes capture outcome value beyond a strong non-adaptive policy.

</details>


### [326] [FractalNet-Based Heterogeneous Federated Learning for Orbital Edge Intelligence in Satellite Mega-Constellations: A Wildfire Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00875)
*Sai Puppala,Koushik Sinha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于FractalNet架构的异构联邦学习方法，用于卫星巨型星座的轨道边缘智能。该方法通过分布式路径调度器根据SWAP-C约束、星间链路和训练统计分配模型深度，并采用三层智能体控制平面管理在轨调度、异常升级和策略自治。以野火检测为案例验证了在不同轨道高度实现分层语义感知的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星巨型星座正成为大规模感知、通信和计算基础设施，但其学习架构仍继承自地面联邦学习和以地面为中心的运营模式，无法适应卫星在SWAP-C（尺寸、重量、功耗和成本）、辐射耐受性、链路可用性和传播延迟上相差数个数量级的独特约束。

Method: 提出基于FractalNet的异构联邦学习方法，包括：(1) 将接触窗口受限和深度异构的联邦优化形式化；(2) 引入分布式路径调度器，根据SWAP-C约束、预测的星间接触和训练统计分配模型深度；(3) 各层级周期性池化更新以减少消息开销和能耗；(4) 三层智能体控制平面管理在轨调度、异常升级和策略治理。

Result: 以野火检测为案例，在不同轨道壳层（LEO/MEO/GEO/HEO）自然学习不同语义层级的情境感知（像素级热异常、区域火线动态、大尺度风险传播）。在模拟巨型星座上的实验验证了方法在收敛性、通信效率、能量适应、调度池化节省、鲁棒性和延迟方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了卫星巨型星座中轨道边缘智能的可行性，通过深度异构联邦学习和分层语义分工，能够适应空间环境的独特资源约束并实现高效的多轨道协同智能。

Abstract: Satellite mega-constellations are emerging as large-scale sensing, communication, and computation fabrics, yet their learning architectures remain largely inherited from terrestrial federated learning and ground-centric mission operations--- ill-suited to satellites that differ by orders of magnitude in Size, Weight, Power, and Cost (SWAP-C), radiation tolerance, link availability, and propagation delay. We propose a heterogeneous federated learning method based on the FractalNet architecture for orbital edge intelligence. We formalize contact-window-constrained, depth-heterogeneous federated optimization and introduce a distributed path scheduler that assigns model depth as a function of SWAP-C constraints, predicted inter-satellite contacts, and training statistics. To reduce message overhead and energy consumption, each tier pools updates periodically rather than at every contact opportunity, and a three-tier agentic control plane governs in-space scheduling, anomaly escalation, and policy-governed autonomy. As a case study, we apply the framework to wildfire detection, where each orbital shell naturally learns a different semantic level of situational awareness: pixel-scale thermal anomalies at low Earth orbit (LEO), regional fire-front dynamics at medium Earth orbit (MEO), and larger-scale risk propagation at geostationary or high Earth orbit (GEO/HEO). Experiments on simulated mega-constellations validate the approach across convergence, communication efficiency, energy adaptation, scheduled-pooling savings, robustness, and latency.

</details>


### [327] [Towards reliable multimodal disaster severity assessment through preference optimization and explainable vision-language reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00879)
*Yuanjun Zhang,Fuzel Ahamed Shaik,Suvojit Acharjee,Fahad Khalid,Mourad Oussalah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段训练框架，将监督微调(SFT)和直接偏好优化(DPO)整合到统一的数据构建流程中，用于提升多模态灾害损害评估模型的准确性和解释质量。实验表明SFT使准确率从73.64%提升至78.29%，Macro-F1提升29%，解释质量提升约25%，后续DPO对齐进一步增强模型解释的可理解性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的灾害损害评估需要模型既能提供准确预测，又能给出透明的解释。然而现有多模态方法面临两个主要挑战：标注数据稀缺，以及对推理质量的评估不足。

Method: 提出一个两阶段训练框架，从单一的人工参与(HITL)标注流程中派生出两个互补数据集：ReasoningSet（包含验证过的推理依据，用于SFT）和PreferenceSet（包含配对的推理依据，用于DPO对齐）。框架同时使用自动指标、基于模型的评分和人工排序来评估分类性能和解释质量。

Result: SFT将准确率从73.64%提升至78.29%，Macro-F1相对基线提升29%，解释质量提升约25%；DPO对齐进一步在PreferenceSet上增强可解释性。跨模型验证（InternVL-3-8B和LLaVA-1.5-7B）证明了方法的鲁棒性和泛化性。框架改善了低代表性轻度损害案例的检测，减少了高风险误分类，并加强了模型推理与人类判断之间的一致性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一条可复现的路径，用于开发可靠的、可审计的、可操作的多模态灾害洞察系统，支持应急管理决策。

Abstract: Reliable disaster damage assessment requires models that provide both accurate predictions and transparent explanations. However, existing multimodal approaches are limited by scarce annotated data and insufficient evaluation of reasoning quality. This study proposes a two-stage training framework that integrates Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) within a unified data construction pipeline. From a single Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) annotation workflow, two complementary datasets are derived, namely ReasoningSet, which contains validated rationales for SFT, and PreferenceSet, which comprises paired rationales for DPO-based alignment. The framework evaluates both classification performance and explanation quality using automatic metrics, model-based scoring, and human ranking. Experimental results show that SFT improves accuracy from 73.64% to 78.29% and increases Macro-F1 by 29% compared to the baseline, while explanation quality improves by approximately 25%. Subsequent DPO alignment further enhances interpretability on the PreferenceSet. Cross-model validation on InternVL-3-8B and LLaVA-1.5-7B demonstrates the robustness and generalizability of the approach. The proposed framework improves detection of underrepresented mild damage cases, reduces high-risk misclassifications, and strengthens alignment between model reasoning and human judgment. Overall, it provides a reproducible pathway to develop reliable multimodal systems that deliver auditable, actionable disaster insights for emergency management.

</details>


### [328] [Denoising Diffusion Generative Models Secretly Calculate Attentions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00885)
*Farzan Haddadi,Leila Monfared,Ebrahim Rezaii,Mohammadreza Malek-Mohammadi,Pejman Zakalvand,Narges Mokhtari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明了扩散模型本质上也在使用与Transformer非常相似的注意力机制，表明注意力是基于通用训练目标而产生的普适机器学习原理。同时发现自编码器与注意力模型在基本功能原理上也有相似性。基于这些等价关系，作者提出了一种基于注意力机制的简化图像生成算法，在显著降低计算资源和训练开销的情况下取得了可比的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型是目前图像生成的主导架构，而自然语言处理主要由基于注意力机制的Transformer架构主导。作者希望揭示这两种看似不同的架构之间的深层联系，从而利用等价性简化图像生成的训练过程并降低计算开销。

Method: 作者首先从理论上证明扩散模型内部蕴含了与Transformer注意力机制高度相似的机制，并论证了自编码器与注意力模型在功能原理上的相似性。基于这些等价关系，将扩散框架重构为注意力机制的形式，并提出一种简化的图像生成算法——用注意力机制替代繁琐的扩散训练过程。

Result: 实验结果表明，提出的基于注意力机制的图像生成方法在生成质量上与扩散模型表现相当（comparable performance），但所需的训练时间和计算资源显著更少。

Conclusion: 注意力机制超越了特定架构的限制，成为基于通用训练目标自然涌现的普适机器学习原理。扩散模型与注意力模型之间的结构等价性使得研究者可以根据实际需求灵活互换不同设计，为高效图像生成提供了新的简化路径。

Abstract: Denoising diffusion models are the dominant architecture for image generation, whereas most natural language generation and modeling are primarily handled by well-known transformer architectures employing attention mechanism. Here, we show that diffusion models also inherently use an attention mechanism very similar to that of transformers. Therefore, attention emerges as a universal machine learning principle, based on a general training objective. We also show similarities in basic functional principle of auto-encoders and attention-based models. These equivalences allows us to interchange these designs based on practical requirements. As an example, we can reformulate the diffusion framework to reduce the lengthy training process and computation-intensive image generation. Using this approach, a simplified algorithm is proposed for image generation which is based on attention mechanism. Results show that the attention-based implementation achieves comparable performance with significantly less effort and computational resources.

</details>


### [329] [CacheBridge: Efficient Cross-Model KV Cache Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00891)
*Xingyu Qu,Siyuan Lu,Zhiyu Chen,Sheng Wang,Tao Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CacheBridge提出了一种跨模型KV缓存迁移的新方法，通过"架构索引的映射器支持、注意力对齐的校准和有界映射器构建"三大协同设计，在保持闭式仿射接口的同时，将每个目标注意力头限制到匹配的源注意力头，并使用注意力因果敏感性加权重构误差，显著降低映射器的存储和应用成本，同时提升迁移质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模型系统中共享上下文需要接收模型重新预填充共享前缀，因为KV缓存是模型特异的。现有的闭式跨模型KV迁移方法（Full-Head Mapping）在选定层级将每个目标KV头映射到所有源KV头，导致迁移质量对架构差异敏感，且映射器的存储和应用开销随层级支持规模增长。这促使需要一种更高效、更稳健的跨模型KV迁移方案。

Method: CacheBridge采用三个协同设计：1) 架构索引的映射器支持，将每个目标注意力头限制到匹配的单个源注意力头；2) 注意力对齐的校准，使用因果注意力敏感性对重构误差进行加权；3) 有界映射器构建，使用融合GPU内核计算加权充分统计量，无需实例化完整的观测张量。同时保持闭式仿射接口以支持在线部署。

Result: 在三个迁移方向上，CacheBridge恢复了Full-Head Mapping在Ministral 3两个迁移方向上失去的大量精度，同时在Qwen3上保持了99.83%的平均目标保留率。在Qwen3 14B到32B的迁移中，映射器存储减少8倍，应用加速最高3.0倍，与Full-Head Mapping使用十分之一校准数据即达到同等效果，500序列构建时间从92.63秒降至8.63秒（加速10.7倍）。

Conclusion: CacheBridge通过头部级别的配对策略替代全头部映射，结合注意力感知的校准加权和高效的GPU内核实现，在减少存储和计算开销的同时提升了跨模型KV迁移的质量和效率，证明了限制性映射设计比全连接的过度参数化映射更为有效，为多模型系统的上下文共享提供了一种更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Sharing context between LLMs in a multi-model system requires the receiving model to prefill the shared prefix because KV caches are model-specific. Recent closed-form cross-model KV transfer, hereafter Full-Head Mapping, avoids this replay by fitting a training-free affine mapper from source to target caches. However, its full-head design maps each target KV head from every source KV head in the selected layers, making transfer quality sensitive to architectural differences and causing mapper storage and application cost to grow with layer support. To this end, we introduce CacheBridge, which co-designs architecture-indexed mapper support, attention-aligned calibration, and bounded mapper construction while retaining a closed-form affine interface for online deployment. CacheBridge restricts each target head to a matched source head, weights reconstruction errors by causal attention sensitivity, and uses a fused GPU kernel to construct weighted sufficient statistics without materializing full observation tensors. Across three transfer directions, CacheBridge recovers the two Ministral 3 transfer directions where Full-Head Mapping loses substantial accuracy while preserving 99.83\% mean target retention on Qwen3. On Qwen3 $14\mathrm{B}\to32\mathrm{B}$, it reduces mapper storage by $8\times$, accelerates application by up to $3.0\times$, matches \fullhead with one tenth of the calibration data, and reduces 500-sequence construction from 92.63 to 8.63 seconds ($10.7\times$).

</details>


### [330] [CARE: Contrastive Anchor-based Rubric Evolution for Large Language Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00892)
*Siyuan Li,Xinxin Song,Chen Ruinian,Jingjing Fan,Tingxiong Xiao,Yangen Hu,Ke Zeng,Jinli Suo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CARE是一种基于对比锚点的法则演化方法，通过在每一步训练中用最高分rollout与前置模型生成的锚点响应对比，结合自适应分支（修复奖励误设）和追逐分支（将前沿质量差距转化为更精细法则），在奖励过优化的高奖励区域维持判别精度，在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于法则的强化学习比基于可验证奖励的强化学习更适合开放式任务的后训练，但静态法则会随策略演化被攻破，而现有动态方法存在法则提取无方向、攻破检测不可靠、法则无限增殖等问题。

Method: CARE在每个训练步骤中，使用前沿模型在提示和法则条件下生成的高质量锚点响应作为基准，将最高分rollout与锚点进行对比，从而实现两个互补机制：自适应分支（反应式修复奖励误设）和追逐分支（主动将前沿质量差距转为更精细的法则）。

Result: 在WildChecklist-9K上用Qwen2.5-7B-Base和Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct的实验表明，CARE在Arena-Hard-2.0、InfoBench和FollowBench上达到SOTA，且是唯一在300步训练中对GPT-4.1锚点响应的胜率持续提升的方法；在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct和Qwen3-8B上进一步验证了其跨模型族的泛化能力。

Conclusion: CARE通过锚点引导的法则演化，在高奖励区域维持判别准确性——这是奖励过优化最主要的来源区域，有效解决了静态法则易被攻破和现有动态方法的三大缺陷，且在不同模型族上均表现良好。

Abstract: Rubric-based reinforcement learning decomposes open-ended instructions into prompt-specific, flexible rubrics, making it better suited than reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards for post-training LLMs on open-ended tasks. However, static rubrics are inevitably hacked as the policy evolves, and existing dynamic approaches introduce new problems: undirected rubric extraction, unreliable hack detection, and unbounded rubric proliferation. We propose $\textbf{CARE}$ ($\textbf{C}$ontrastive $\textbf{A}$nchor-based $\textbf{R}$ubric $\textbf{E}$volution), which grounds every rubric evolution step in a high-quality anchor response generated by a frontier model conditioned on the prompt and its rubrics. At each training step, CARE contrasts the highest-scoring rollout against the anchor, enabling two complementary mechanisms: an Adaptive branch that reactively repairs reward misspecification; and a Chase branch that proactively converts frontier-level quality gaps into sharper rubrics. Together, the two branches $\textbf{maintain discriminative accuracy in the high-reward region}$---the precise region where reward over-optimization mostly originates. Experiments on WildChecklist-9K with Qwen2.5-7B-Base and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct show that CARE achieves state-of-the-art performance on Arena-Hard-2.0, InfoBench, and FollowBench, and is the $\textbf{only}$ method whose win rate against GPT-4.1 anchor responses shows sustained improvement throughout 300 training steps; additional results on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct and Qwen3-8B further indicate that CARE generalizes across model families.

</details>


### [331] [In-Context Neurofeedback: Can LLMs Control Their Internal Representations through Privileged Access?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00904)
*Koshiro Aoki,Ryota Takatsuki,Gouki Minegishi,Yusuke Haruki,Daisuke Kawahara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过重新设计神经反馈范式，使其满足人类认知神经科学中的特权访问要求，发现大语言模型无法可靠控制特权内部表征，表明先前报道的模型内部控制可能依赖于表层机制而非真正的元认知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究声称LLM可通过神经反馈控制自身内部表征，但其控制目标不具备特权性（第三方可从提示中推断），无法排除模型依赖表层机制的可能性。因此需要在更严格的特权访问条件下重新评估LLM的内部控制能力。

Method: 重新设计LLM的神经反馈范式，使控制目标满足特权访问要求（即无法从提示中由第三方推断），该设置更接近人类认知神经科学中的神经反馈实验，并在此严格条件下测试模型对特权内部表征的控制能力。

Result: 在满足特权访问要求的更严格设置下，模型无法表现出对特权内部表征的可靠控制，表明先前报告的控制效果无法排除其依赖表层机制的可能性。

Conclusion: 对LLM元认知能力的严格评估需要采用要求特权访问的评价方法，否则可能高估模型的内部控制/元认知能力。

Abstract: Whether large language models (LLMs) can control their own internal representations matters for both machine metacognition and AI safety. A recent study applied neurofeedback to LLMs and claimed that they can control their internal representations. However, the reported control may rely on superficial mechanisms rather than genuine internal access because the control targets in that study are not privileged, meaning that a third party can infer them from the prompt. We redesign the neurofeedback paradigm for LLMs so that the control target satisfies the privileged access requirement, which is closer to neurofeedback experiments in human cognitive neuroscience. Under this stricter setting, the models do not demonstrate reliable control over privileged internal representations, suggesting that previously reported control cannot exclude the possibility that it relies on superficial mechanisms. Our results indicate that rigorous assessments of metacognition in LLMs require evaluation methods that demand privileged access.

</details>


### [332] [RPCBench: A Benchmark for Proactive Premise Critique in LLM-based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00918)
*Zhongru Chen,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RPCBench基准，用于评估大型语言模型作为交互式推荐助手时，能否检测、诊断并正确处理自然语言推荐请求中有缺陷的前提（即Recommender-Premise Critique能力）。该基准涵盖5个推荐领域、10种前提失败类型，并通过细粒度评估框架对11个LLM进行系统评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐基准主要评估排序、生成或偏好满足，而现有错误检测基准又缺乏推荐场景中用户与候选证据的支撑。随着LLM越来越多地用作交互式推荐助手，有必要评估它们能否识别存在缺陷的推荐请求，而不仅仅是生成看似合理的推荐。

Method: 构建RPCBench基准，包含基于证据的测试实例，覆盖5个推荐领域和10种前提失败类型。每个实例提供可见的推荐上下文和损坏的用户查询。设计细粒度评估框架，从主动检测、错误定位、检测后处理策略和证据忠实性四个维度进行评测。对11个LLM进行系统评估。

Result: 主动检测是推荐前提批评能力的主要瓶颈；模型在欠规格前提错误（underspecified-premise errors）上表现最差；目标关键信息密度比冗余证据更重要；较长的推理并不会单调提升批评质量——性能在中等推理长度时达到峰值，而过长的推理伴随过思考惩罚。

Conclusion: RPCBench填补了推荐场景下错误检测基准的空白，揭示了LLM在推荐前提批评中的主要短板（主动检测和欠规格前提处理），并为提升模型在交互式推荐中的可靠性提供了方向性指导。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as interactive recommender assistants. Their evaluation should therefore go beyond plausible item recommendation and test whether they can recognize flawed recommendation requests. Existing recommender benchmarks mainly assess ranking, generation, or preference satisfaction, while existing error-detection benchmarks are usually not grounded in recommendation-specific user and candidate evidence. To address this gap, we introduce RPCBench, a benchmark for evaluating Recommender-Premise Critique: the ability to detect, diagnose, and properly handle faulty premises in natural-language recommendation requests. RPCBench contains evidence-grounded test instances from five recommendation domains and covers ten types of premise failures. Each instance provides a visible recommendation context and a corrupted user query. We further design a fine-grained evaluation framework that measures proactive detection, error localization, post-detection handling strategy, and evidence faithfulness. Through a systematic evaluation of 11 LLMs, we find that proactive detection is the main bottleneck in Recommender-Premise Critique, and models perform worst on underspecified-premise errors. We also observe that target-critical information density matters more than redundant evidence, and that longer reasoning does not monotonically improve critique quality: performance peaks at intermediate reasoning length, while overly long reasoning is accompanied by an overthinking penalty. The code is available at https://github.com/ZhongruChen/RPCBench.

</details>


### [333] [VIBE-Bench: Evaluating Personalized Large Language Models When Profiles Don't Mean Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00921)
*Yiwen Jiang,Yang Deng,Stephanie Fong,Zimu Wang,Yaling Shen,Wei Feng,Hongxi Yang,Xiangyu Zhao,Zhongxing Xu,Deval Mehta,Xuelian Cheng,Zongyuan Ge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VIBE-Bench基准，聚焦个性化大语言模型中"画像偏好概念错位"(PRCM)这一未充分研究的挑战——用户的画像线索与查询特定偏好处于不同概念空间，现有模型依赖表面语义相关性进行偏好检索，无法进行跨概念推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PLLM基准大多假设偏好可从语义相关的历史记录中检索，但在实际场景中，用户画像线索（如性格特征）与查询相关的具体偏好（如兴趣选择）往往分属不同概念空间，导致语义检索在个性化中失效。这一概念错位领域缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 作者构建了VIBE-Bench基准，包含两个基于心理学理论设计的任务、3,504个人格画像和12,239个对话样本，并配有手工验证的金标准测试集，要求模型进行跨概念偏好推理而非依赖表面语义重叠。

Result: 实验表明，现有PLLM个性化方法严重依赖浅层语义相关性，在VIBE-Bench上无法习得稳健的跨概念映射，暴露出跨概念推理能力的显著不足。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了PRCM作为PLLM中一个独特的失败场景，并将VIBE-Bench定位为推动偏好推理超越语义匹配的专项测试平台。

Abstract: Personalized Large Language Models (PLLMs) aim to tailor responses to individual users, where a central challenge is preference reasoning: inferring query-relevant preferences from user-related history. Existing benchmarks, however, largely assume that such preference can be retrieved from semantically related history. We study an underexplored but practically important regime, profile-preference conceptual misalignment (PRCM), where observable profile cues and query-specific preferences lie in different concept spaces, making semantic retrieval inconsistent for personalization. We introduce VIBE-Bench, a benchmark with two psychology-grounded tasks, 3,504 personas and 12,239 dialogues, including a manually verified gold test set, and requires cross-concept preference reasoning beyond surface semantic overlap. Experiments with several personalization methods show that current PLLMs largely rely on shallow semantic correlations and fail to acquire robust cross-concept mappings. These findings establish PRCM as a distinct failure regime in PLLMs and position VIBE-Bench as a focused testbed for advancing preference reasoning beyond semantic matching.

</details>


### [334] [Few-Shot Out of Domain Intent Detection with Covariance Corrected Mahalanobis Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00961)
*Jayasimha Talur,Oleg Smirnov,Paul Missault*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对少样本场景下Mahalanobis距离在OOD意图检测中性能不佳的问题，分析其原因并提出一种协方差校正的Mahalanobis距离方法来提升检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Podolskiy等人提出的Mahalanobis距离方法在OOD意图检测中表现优异，但在实际重要的少样本（few-shot）设置下无法超越基线方法，需要分析性能低下的原因并加以改进。

Method: 分析Mahalanobis距离在少样本设置下性能不佳的深层原因，并提出一种协方差校正（covariance corrected）的Mahalanobis距离方法来检测OOD意图。

Result: 协方差校正的Mahalanobis距离方法在少样本OOD意图检测中表现优于基线方法（具体详细实验结果未在摘要中给出）。

Conclusion: 通过协方差校正策略有效解决了少样本场景下Mahalanobis距离方法性能退化的问题，为实际应用中的OOD意图检测提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Conversational agents like chatbots and voice assistants are trained to understand and respond to user intents. On encountering an utterance with an intent different from the ones they have been trained on, these agents are expected to classify the intent as `unknown' or `out of domain'. This problem is known as out of domain (OOD) intent detection. Podolskiy et al. (2021), showed that Mahalanobis distance can be used effectively for identifying OOD intents, outperforming competing approaches. However, their method fails to outperform the baselines in the practically important few-shot setting. In this paper we analyze the reason for low performance and propose a covariance corrected Mahalanobis distance for detecting out-of-domain intents.

</details>


### [335] [CoBRA: Learning Tool-Use Boundaries via Counterfactual Margins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00967)
*Wenhao Zou,Xianglong Liu,Wendong Bi,Hanjie Wang,Simin Zhao,Gong Zhi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoBRA框架，通过学习反事实边界来决定大语言模型何时需要调用外部工具，以提高工具使用效率和答案准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法基于难度、置信度或任务奖励等绝对信号来触发工具调用，缺乏对每次调用边际收益的显式估计，导致不必要的工具调用（带来延迟、成本、噪声传播）或遗漏必要调用（损害知识密集型查询）。

Method: CoBRA包含两个阶段：首先从同一基础模型构建内部和外部专家，收集配对轨迹并估计有无工具之间的奖励边际，将数据划分为内部偏好、外部偏好和模糊三类；然后利用清晰边际样本进行边界感知冷启动SFT，再通过MARS-RL（参考分割rollout与反事实边际优势）优化边界决策。

Result: 以检索为主要工具在Qwen3-4B上的实验表明，CoBRA提升了工具使用效率和边界敏感型答案的准确率，同时在工具依赖的分布外问题上保持了强劲性能。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模单次工具调用的反事实边际收益并学习决策边界，CoBRA能够在准确性和效率之间取得更好的权衡。

Abstract: As large language models increasingly act through external tools, deciding when to call a tool has become a central problem alongside deciding how to use it. Unnecessary tool calls introduce latency, cost, retrieval noise, and error propagation, while missed calls hurt knowledge-intensive queries or questions requiring up-to-date evidence. Existing methods typically trigger tools from absolute query or generation signals, such as difficulty, confidence, or final task reward, and therefore lack an explicit estimate of the instance-level marginal benefit of tool use. We propose CoBRA, a counterfactual boundary-learning framework for tool-augmented language models. CoBRA first constructs internal and external experts from the same base model, collects paired trajectories, and estimates the reward margin between answering with and without tools. This margin partitions data into internal-favored, external-favored, and ambiguous cases. CoBRA then uses clear-margin samples for Boundary-Aware Cold-Start SFT, followed by MARS-RL with reference-split rollouts and counterfactual marginal advantages to optimize boundary decisions. Experiments with retrieval as the main tool on Qwen3-4B show that CoBRA improves tool-use efficiency and boundary-sensitive answer accuracy while maintaining strong performance on tool-dependent out-of-distribution questions.

</details>


### [336] [Figures as Programs: Recursive Generation of Editable Scientific Figures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01006)
*Yepeng Liu,Dasen Dai,Chengzhi Liu,Yiren Song,Hai Ci,Yu Zhang,Qi Zhang,Mike Zheng Shou,Xin Eric Wang,Yuheng Bu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FigTree，一个多智能体系统，将科学论文自动转化为结构化矢量图。系统将图表生成视为递归SVG程序构建，通过将图表分解为层次化的局部区域、生成短SVG程序并组装，再结合渲染-评论循环进行缺陷追溯与修复，实现高质量且可编辑的科学图表生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学方法学图表对清晰传达复杂方法至关重要，但其创建过程劳动密集且需多轮修改。现有图像生成模型虽能合成美观的栅格图，但难以单步生成令人满意的结果，且对栅格图进行精确编辑对人和模型均具挑战性。

Method: FigTree将科学图表生成重构为递归SVG程序构建。系统基于源论文锚定图表内容，将图表分解为层次化的局部区域，将每个区域生成为短SVG程序片段，并组装生成完整矢量图。通过渲染-评论（render-critic）循环同时检查渲染图和底层程序，将视觉缺陷追溯至具体代码语句并精确修复。

Result: 大量实验评估表明，FigTree在图表质量和可编辑性方面均表现优异：能生成高质量图表，且相较现有基于栅格的方法实现更有效的编辑能力。

Conclusion: 将科学图表建模为结构化矢量程序而非栅格图像，结合多智能体分解与渲染-评论迭代优化，是生成高质量且易于编辑的科学图表的有效路径。

Abstract: Scientific methodology figures are essential for communicating complex methods clearly, yet creating them remains labor-intensive and typically requires multiple rounds of refinement. Recent image-generation models can synthesize visually appealing raster figures, but producing a human-satisfactory result in a single generation step remains difficult. Moreover, precise edits to raster figures are challenging for both humans and models. We formulate scientific figure generation as recursive SVG program construction and propose \textsc{FigTree}, a \textit{multi-agent} system that automatically transforms a scientific paper into a structured vector figure. \textsc{FigTree} grounds figure content in the source paper, decomposes a figure into a hierarchy of local regions, generates each region as a short SVG program, and assembles the resulting fragments. A render-critic refinement loop jointly inspects the rendered figure and its underlying program, enabling visual defects to be traced to specific statements and accurately repaired. We conduct extensive evaluations of \textsc{FigTree} on figure quality and editability, showing that \textsc{FigTree} produces high-quality figures, while also enabling more effective editing than existing raster-based methods.

</details>


### [337] [Spawn Freely, Act Sparingly: Progressive Risk Vesting for Recursive LLM-Agent Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01035)
*Molly Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"渐进风险归属"(PRV)框架，通过在托管账户中持有轨迹级风险预算并对分支激活进行扣款，来管理递归LLM代理中分支被授予执行权限时的风险。论文证明了自适应生成树的任意时刻危害界，并揭示了风险跨越临界点(权威再生数R_A=1)时的相变行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归LLM代理通过生成专家分支来扩展搜索能力，但某些分支会请求发送数据或部署代码的工具调用。核心问题是：分支何时应被授予行动权限？现有方法缺乏对适应性生成树结构中风险的系统性管理机制，需要一个理论框架来约束跨分支激活时的累积危害。

Method: 引入"渐进风险归属"(PRV)——在托管账户中持有轨迹级风险预算，分支激活时逐次扣减。区分沙盒生成(外部控制防止特定危害)与能力激活(所选分支越过不可逆行动边界)。证明在激活门控、分支收费和计算约束固定的情况下，延迟归属保留了不可撤销生成收费下的所有策略。还提出了有限类型占用模型，推导风险与计算影子价格，以及嵌套扇出模式下的阈值规则。

Result: 证明了自适应生成树的任意时刻危害界。发现轨迹危害在权威再生数R_A跨越1时发生相变：当局部风险p趋近零时，临界以下危害正比于p，临界处正比于√p，临界以上则保持正的下限。分支计算和分裂样本实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 设计规则建议：在沙盒中广泛搜索，谨慎授予递归权限，并引入明确的风险收费。论文强调这些合成研究并不估计部署代理中的实际安全性。

Abstract: Recursive LLM agents can broaden their search by spawning specialists. Some branches later request tools that send data or deploy code. When should a branch receive authority to act? We distinguish sandbox spawning, in which external controls prevent the specified harm, from capability activation, in which a selected branch crosses an irreversible-action boundary. Progressive Risk Vesting (PRV) holds a trajectory-level risk budget in escrow and debits it as branches are activated. We prove an anytime harm bound for adaptively generated trees. Branch outcomes may be dependent, but each local certificate needs to remain valid conditional on the full pre-activation history, including the information used to select the request. When activation gates, branch charges, and compute constraints are held fixed, delayed vesting preserves every policy available under irrevocable spawn charging. Marginal risk estimates can still fail after branch selection. In a stylized branching model, trajectory harm changes as the authority reproduction number $\mathcal{R}_A$ crosses one. As local risk $p$ approaches zero, trajectory harm is proportional to $p$ below criticality, proportional to $\sqrt{p}$ at criticality, and retains a positive floor above it. A finite-type occupancy model yields risk and compute shadow prices. For nested fanout modes with decreasing marginal value per unit risk, these prices produce a threshold rule. Branching calculations and a split-sample experiment illustrate the results. These synthetic studies do not estimate safety in deployed agents. The analysis suggests a design rule: search broadly in the sandbox and grant recursive authority sparingly, with an explicit risk charge.

</details>


### [338] [Data-Driven Persona-Conditioned Agents for A/B Test Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01038)
*Ziyad Benomar,Weronika Łajewska,Leonardo Perelli,Saab Mansour*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用LLM智能体模拟A/B测试结果的仿真框架，基于数据驱动的用户画像（由真实匿名行为数据构建），在40个A/B测试基准上实现了0.75-0.90的方向准确率，为快速、低成本的实验预筛选提供了可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统A/B测试需要真实用户流量、工程投入和数周测量周期，成本高昂且效率低下；而先前使用合成或基于规则的画像建模工作无法忠实反映真实用户群体，需要一种更高效且更准确的实验预筛选方法。

Method: 提出一种仿真框架，利用LLM驱动的智能体来预测A/B测试结果。智能体基于数据驱动的用户画像构建，画像来源于匿名化的真实用户行为数据（活动模式、参与信号、推断的人口统计数据）。将A/B测试仿真建模为结构化问答任务，系统研究了四方面问题：问题设计格式、画像数据来源与领域对齐的影响、单画像行为深度与群体多样性之间的权衡、以及高效群体子采样策略。

Result: 在涵盖两种指标类型的40个A/B测试基准上，最优配置的方向准确率根据测试指标不同达到0.75-0.90，证明了数据驱动画像在实验预筛选中的有效性。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的LLM智能体画像能够作为传统A/B测试的有效补充，实现快速、低成本的效果预筛，为产品和功能迭代决策提供了新的技术途径。

Abstract: A/B testing is the gold standard for evaluating product changes, but each experiment requires real user traffic, engineering effort, and weeks of measurement. We propose a simulation framework that predicts A/B test outcomes using LLM-powered agents conditioned on data-driven personas grounded in real user behavioral signals. Unlike prior work that relies on synthetic or rule-based personas, our agents are constructed from anonymized behavioral data-activity patterns, engagement signals, and inferred demographics-enabling more faithful population modeling. We frame A/B test simulation as a structured question task and systematically study (i) question design formats, (ii) the impact of persona data source and domain alignment, (iii) the trade-off between per-persona behavioral depth and population diversity, and (iv) efficient population subsampling. On a benchmark of 40 A/B tests spanning two metric types, our best configuration achieves 0.75-0.90 directional accuracy depending on the test metric, demonstrating that data-driven personas are a viable path toward fast, low-cost experiment pre-screening.

</details>


### [339] [AgentFactory: Towards Automated Agentic System Design and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01045)
*Enci Zhang,Haofeng Wang,Yuesheng Zhu,Xiaole Cui,Guibo Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentFactory是一个联合优化基础模型与工作流结构的智能体系统框架，同时考虑性能、成本和效率等多重目标，通过三阶段优化管线自动发现微调模型与优化工作流的有效组合。在覆盖五个领域的八个基准测试上，平均提升9.1%，领域特定任务上提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体系统设计主要依赖人工设计和优化，缺乏适应性和可扩展性。已有自动优化方法往往忽视模型能力的作用，仅关注单一性能指标，无法满足实际部署中多目标约束（如成本、效率）的需求。

Method: AgentFactory利用高级LLM作为优化器来导航庞大的配置搜索空间，采用三阶段优化管线，通过迭代优化过程系统性地探索和评估不同的智能体系统设计，结合微调模型与优化工作流，同时优化多个目标（性能、成本、效率）。

Result: 在涉及通用推理、编码、数学、医学和金融五个领域的八个基准测试中，AgentFactory相比人工设计方法和现有自动化方法均有稳定提升，平均提升9.1%，其中在MedQA上提升19.6%，在FinEval上提升18.7%。

Conclusion: AgentFactory通过自动化联合优化，为开发更具能力且更高效的智能体系统提供了有前景的途径，验证了同时考虑模型与工作流优化、以及多目标约束的有效性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities as powerful components in agentic systems, enabling sophisticated reasoning and complex task execution. However, current approaches to manually designing and optimizing agentic systems heavily rely on manual effort, limiting their adaptability and scalability. Recent work has explored the automated optimization of workflow designs. However, these approaches often overlook the crucial role of model capabilities and focus on single performance metrics, failing to address real-world deployment constraints. In this paper, we present AgentFactory, a framework that jointly optimizes both foundation models and workflow structures in agentic systems while considering multiple objectives including performance, cost, and efficiency. AgentFactory leverages advanced LLMs as optimizers to navigate the vast search space of possible configurations, employing a three-stage optimization pipeline to automatically discover effective combinations of fine-tuned models and optimized workflows. Through an iterative optimization process, our framework systematically explores and evaluates different agentic system designs, adapting to task-specific requirements while maintaining operational efficiency. We evaluate AgentFactory across eight benchmarks spanning five domains, including general reasoning, coding, mathematics, medicine, and finance. Our experiments demonstrate that AgentFactory consistently outperforms both manually designed methods and existing automated approaches, achieving an average improvement of 9.1% across all benchmarks, with particularly significant gains in domain-specific tasks (19.6% on MedQA and 18.7% on FinEval). These results establish AgentFactory as a promising approach for developing more capable and efficient agentic systems through automated optimization.

</details>


### [340] [QILP-0: Constructing Observational Declarative Twins of Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01049)
*Marina de la Cruz Echeandía,César Luis Alonso,Tony Ribeiro,Alfonso Ortega de la Puente*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出QXymb框架及其首个实现QILP-0，通过从量子电路观测行为中构造有限多值命题逻辑程序，实现量子电路的精确可观测声明性孪生，并在两套QML实验中达到完全无冲突的严格精确重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电路的行为建模长期以来依赖数值近似和黑箱后端，缺乏一种能够精确捕获可观测行为、同时将逻辑精确性与数值和离散化不确定性分离的通用声明性框架。

Method: QXymb通过结构化分级遍历量子可观测族，以参考相对覆盖率量化进展，经目标无关几何组织可观测响应，并保留潜在结构映射回原始可观测列。选定的观测轮廓经目标无关离散化转为有限关系，目标仅在后续用于审计孪生可接受性和归纳声明性理论。

Result: 在Bars & Stripes（16-100比特，原生离散分支）和Low-Depth MNIST（全部14,708个0/1数字实例，连续离散化）实验中，QILP-0均实现完全无冲突重建且严格精度为1。

Conclusion: QILP-0证明所提出的方法能将逻辑精确性与数值、后端、提供者和离散化不确定性分离，为量子电路的可观测行为提供可认证的精确声明性孪生。

Abstract: This paper introduces QXymb, a general framework for constructing observational declarative twins of quantum circuits, and develops QILP-0, its first complete order-0 specialization. QILP-0 constructs a finite multi-valued propositional logic program from observed circuit behaviour within a declared observational scope.
  The pipeline traverses a declared family of quantum observables incrementally according to a reproducible structural grading and a declared observational reference horizon. Progress is quantified through reference-relative coverage against a fixed target-independent reference. Observable responses are organized through target-independent geometry, while retained latent structure is mapped deterministically back to original observable columns before symbolic processing, preserving observational semantics and provenance.
  Selected observable profiles are converted into a finite relation through admissible target-independent discretization. The target is used only afterwards to audit twin-admissibility and induce the declarative theory. A theory is certified as an exact observational declarative twin when it completely and correctly reconstructs the resulting finite task-conditioned discrete relation. Logical exactness is therefore separated from numerical, backend, provider, and discretization uncertainty, which is retained as audit metadata.
  Validation uses two complementary QML settings. Exhaustive Bars & Stripes experiments compare product and grid-CZ embeddings from 16 to 100 qubits and exercise the native-discrete branch. Low-Depth MNIST analyses all 14,708 digit-0/1 instances before and after a trained variational quantum transformation and exercises continuous discretization. In every reported relation, the induced QILP-0 theory achieves complete, conflict-free reconstruction with strict accuracy equal to one.

</details>


### [341] [WorldBench: Culturally Grounded Benchmark for Multilingual Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01056)
*Leonardo Ranaldi,Sherrie Shen,Jushi Kai,Alexandra Birch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WorldBench是一个涵盖7种语言、8种文化的多语言基准测试，包含1,600个基于真实人物设定的日常多步任务。实验发现前沿模型仅达到49.2%的CTS分数，且在正确性与环境保持之间存在显著差距，表明当前智能体在多语言、长时程场景下依然脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM智能体基准测试很少评估状态保持能力、跨语言表现以及对真实场景的适用性，缺乏一个全面的多语言、基于真实人物设定的工作流基准。

Method: 构建WorldBench基准：包含1,600个任务，覆盖7种语言和8种文化，通过具有语言和文化专长的人工标注者筛选和精炼；提出Constrained Task Success (CTS)指标，结合自然语言指令和测试环境，通过确定性和LLM-as-a-Judge评估来综合衡量任务完成度、最小变更度及其他补充指标。

Result: 前沿模型在CTS上仅达到49.2%，所有模型在正确性与环境保持之间都展现出巨大差距，尤其表现在长时程任务和需要状态保持约束的场景中。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体在多语言智能体场景中仍然不够稳健，特别是在长时程任务和状态保持约束下表现较弱，需要进一步改进。

Abstract: Despite the growing use of LLM-powered agents to solve multi-step tasks in complex environments, existing benchmarks rarely test state preservation, performance across languages, and application to realistic, grounded scenarios. To address these concerns, we present WorldBench: a comprehensive, multilingual benchmark of genuine, persona-grounded everyday workflows, where agents can act in a sandbox via structured actions. WorldBench comprises 1,600 tasks across seven languages and eight cultures, filtered and refined through feedback from human annotators with language- and culture-specific expertise. For evaluation, we extend metrics from previous works and introduce Constrained Task Success (CTS), which combines natural language instructions and testbeds to score task completion, minimal modification, and other complementary metrics through deterministic and LLM-as-a-Judge evaluations. Our experiments show that frontier models reach only 49.2% CTS, with all models demonstrating large gaps between correctness and environment preservation. We thereby show that current agents remain brittle in multilingual, agentic scenarios, especially for long-horizon tasks and under state-preservation constraints

</details>


### [342] [User Representation via Cross Multi-source Behavior Pre-training for Mobile Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01057)
*Chengqi Yang,Yiran Qiao,Feng Liu,Xingyu Lou,Zijun Zhou,Xiaoyun Mo,Changwang Zhang,Jiayuan Xu,Jun Wang,Xiang Ao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CM-PTM，一种面向移动设备级行为日志的跨多源行为预训练模型，用于移动游戏用户表示学习。通过层叠掩码预测代理任务，先推断下一行为的来源，再逐步细化到应用-动作级别，从而在统一预训练框架中建模跨源依赖和细粒度行为动态，在下游移动游戏推荐任务中取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户表示预训练研究主要聚焦于单一应用或应用级别的行为建模，忽视了移动设备上用户活动固有的跨来源性和多粒度特性。设备层面上，用户意图源于异构行为来源与分层动作结构之间的复杂交互，传统以应用为中心的建模方法无法有效解决这一挑战。

Method: 提出CM-PTM模型，采用层级级联的掩码-预测代理任务：首先推断下一行为的来源（cross-source），然后逐步细化预测到应用-动作（app-action）级别。通过这种级联设计，在统一的预训练范式内实现跨源依赖与细粒度行为动态的联合建模。

Result: 在大规模真实移动设备数据集上的广泛实验表明，CM-PTM能够有效捕捉用户内生兴趣，并在下游移动游戏推荐任务中持续带来显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: CM-PTM验证了设备级跨多源行为预训练的有效性，为解决多源异质行为建模难题提供了新思路，能够更好地服务于移动游戏等下游个性化任务。

Abstract: User representation pre-training has become a fundamental paradigm for alleviating data sparsity in downstream personalization tasks. However, existing studies predominantly focus on single-app or app-level behaviors, overlooking the inherently cross-source and multi-granular nature of user activities on mobile devices. At the device level, user intent emerges from complex interactions among heterogeneous behavior sources and hierarchical action structures, posing challenges that cannot be addressed by conventional app-centric modeling. To tackle this issue, we propose CM-PTM, a novel Cross Multi-source Behavior Pre-Training Model tailored for mobile game user representation learning on device-level behavioral logs. CM-PTM employs hierarchical cascaded mask-then-predict proxy tasks that first infer the source of the next behavior and then progressively refine predictions at the app-action level. This design enables unified modeling of cross-source dependencies and fine-grained behavioral dynamics within a single pre-training paradigm. Extensive experiments on large-scale real-world mobile datasets demonstrate that CM-PTM effectively captures users' endogenous interests and consistently delivers significant performance gains on downstream mobile game recommendation tasks.

</details>


### [343] [ARISE-RL: Agentic Rubric-Grounded Iterative Self-Evolution with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01058)
*Fanrui Zhang,Ruixue Ding,Qiang Zhang,Xi Chen,Boli Chen,Shihang Wang,Qiuchen Wang,Hongmin Zhan,Jinxin Bian,Li xingchao,Peijin Zheng,Hao cheng,Pengjun Xie,Kaipeng Zhang,Jiawei Liu,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ARISE-RL，一个全周期自我进化框架，通过任务/评分标准生成器与推理求解器的协同进化解决开放式智能体RL训练中奖励稀疏和不稳定的问题，并引入RG-SED蒸馏策略和ECR-Bench基准套件，实现跨基准的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式智能体的强化学习训练面临两大挑战：一是缺乏可验证的黄金答案和可扩展的评分标准；二是长时程开放式任务中奖励脆弱不稳定，导致组策略学习中对比信号弱或噪声大，难以捕捉细粒度优化信号。

Method: ARISE-RL包含三个关键组件：(1) Generator-Solver共进化框架，Generator基于真实工具观测生成有效的中等难度任务和评分标准，Solver从细粒度评分满足信号中学习；(2) Reward-Gated Self-Evolution Distillation (RG-SED)，仅在记忆增强变体带来实际奖励提升时选择性地自我蒸馏，减少分布偏移并避免盲目模仿；(3) ECR-Bench基准套件，涵盖单工具深度研究和多工具旅行规划，用于专家校准的严格评估。

Result: 大量实验表明ARISE-RL在所有评估基准上一致地实现了稳健且稳定的整体最优性能(state-of-the-art)。

Conclusion: 通过评分标准中介的共进化、奖励门控的自我蒸馏以及专家校准的基准评估，ARISE-RL为开放式智能体训练提供了完整有效的解决方案，解决了奖励不稳定性这一核心瓶颈。

Abstract: Training open-ended agents via reinforcement learning (RL) is hindered by the lack of verifiable gold answers and scalable rubrics. Moreover, even near the model's capability boundary, long-horizon open-ended agentic tasks often yield brittle and unstable rewards, resulting in weak or noisy rollout contrast that obscures fine-grained optimization signals for group-based policy learning. To address these challenges, we propose ARISE-RL, a novel full-cycle self-evolution framework that couples a task/rubric Generator and a reasoning Solver through rubric-mediated co-evolution. The Generator grounds tool-related rubric criteria in real tool observations and is rewarded for producing valid, intermediate-difficulty tasks aligned with the Solver's evolving capability boundary. The Solver, in turn, learns from fine-grained rubric satisfaction signals through multi-step reasoning and tool use. We further introduce Reward-Gated Self-Evolution Distillation (RG-SED), which selectively distills a memory-augmented variant of the same policy back into itself only when the memory yields empirical reward improvement, thereby reducing distribution mismatch and avoiding blind imitation of noisy guidance. Finally, to support rigorous evaluation, we present ECR-Bench, an expert-calibrated rubric benchmark suite covering single-tool deep research and multi-tool travel planning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ARISE-RL consistently achieves robust and stable overall state-of-the-art performance across all evaluated benchmarks.

</details>


### [344] [Space Generative AI with Solar Energy Harvesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01062)
*Jierui Zhang,Jianhao Huang,Zhanwei Wang,Kaibin Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个太阳能供电的空间生成式AI框架，卫星在严格时间窗口内接收用户提示、执行基于扩散模型的图像生成并下行压缩结果，通过联合优化计算-通信资源以最大化端到端生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星有望将生成式AI服务扩展到缺乏地面基础设施的偏远地区，但空间AI部署受限于太阳能采集提供的有限且时变的机载能量，需要解决计算与通信共享能量预算下的根本性权衡问题。

Method: 利用确定性轨道运动导致的可预测太阳能采集动态，构建联合计算-通信资源优化框架，采用两步法：首先在固定生成深度下刻画连续太阳能采集的最大下行吞吐量，建立分离原理解耦等待时间选择与发射功率控制；随后建立联合效用最大化问题，在恒定功率主导区间推导出闭式低复杂度的步数选择策略。

Result: 在真实轨道动力学下的大量实验表明，所提策略能动态平衡生成质量与传输可靠性，在多种太阳能采集状态下相比静态计算中心和通信中心基线取得显著的端到端性能增益。

Conclusion: 该框架验证了利用太阳能采集可预测性进行联合计算-通信资源优化的可行性，为受限能源条件下空间生成式AI服务提供了有效的低复杂度解决方案。

Abstract: Satellites are emerging as promising platforms to extend generative \emph{artificial intelligence} (AI) services to remote areas lacking terrestrial infrastructure. However, deploying space generative AI is fundamentally constrained by the limited, time-varying onboard energy supplied by solar \emph{energy harvesting} (EH). This paper presents a framework for solar-powered space generative AI in which a satellite receives a user prompt, executes a diffusion-based image-generation model, and downlinks the compressed result within a strict time window. We identify the fundamental \emph{computation--communication} (C$^2$) trade-offs governed by the shared harvested-energy budgets. Specifically, increasing the number of generation steps improves intrinsic image quality but depletes energy and time available for downlink transmission, whereas prioritizing communication guarantees reliable delivery but sacrifices semantic quality. To balance these trade-offs and maximize \emph{end-to-end} (E2E) generative performance, we exploit the predictable solar-EH dynamics induced by deterministic orbital motion and develop a joint C$^2$ resource-optimization framework using a tractable two-step approach. First, we characterize the maximum downlink throughput for a fixed generation depth under continuous solar EH. This establishes a separation principle that decouples waiting-time selection from optimal transmit-power control. Next, we formulate a joint C$^2$ utility-maximization problem and derive a closed-form, low-complexity step-selection policy in the dominant constant-power regime. Extensive experiments under realistic orbital dynamics demonstrate that the proposed policy dynamically balances generation quality and transmission reliability. This yields significant E2E performance gains over static computation- and communication-centric baselines across diverse solar-EH states.

</details>


### [345] [Latent Recurrent Thoughts: Recurrent Refinement of Proposed Latents for Reasoning with Frozen LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01117)
*Zhaoliang Chen,Jie Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent Recurrent Thoughts (LRT)方法，在连续表示空间中进行推理：保持一个大型语言模型（LLM）冻结，仅用于建模和解码序列，由一个小型辅助网络通过循环递推产生连续的潜在思维作为输入，从而解耦计算深度与模型规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链式思维（Chain-of-Thought）推理在离散token空间中展开存在固有缺陷：每步以文本形式提交，错误会传播累积，且获得好的推理轨迹前提是需要有可模仿的轨迹。在模型的连续表示空间中进行推理可以避免这些限制。

Method: LRT包含三个组件：一个任务专用proposer提供基础潜在表示，一个循环reasoner通过有界残差修正反复细化这些潜在状态，最后冻结的LLM解码答案。循环reasoner使推理深度与模型规模解耦，潜在状态通过多次迭代处理产生而非单次前向传播。

Result: 在符号推理任务（Countdown-4, Sudoku）和自然语言推理任务（HumanEval, MBPP, StrategyQA）上，LRT在相同的解码器、提示词、数据和训练预算下显著优于先前的冻结解码器连续空间推理方法，并以较小的推理计算量超越了同骨干网络上不使用思考模式的链式思维提示。

Conclusion: 在连续潜在空间中通过循环精炼实现推理是可行且高效的，既能规避离散token空间推理的错误传播问题，又能以较小的计算开销获得优于或媲美链式思维推理的性能。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought reasoning unfolds in discrete token space: each step is committed as text, errors propagate, and eliciting good traces presupposes traces to imitate. Reasoning instead in a model's continuous representation space - where intermediate states are vectors rather than words - sidesteps these constraints, but leaves open how those latent states should be computed. We approach this along two axes. First, we keep a large language model (LLM) frozen and use it for what it is already good at - modeling and decoding sequences - while a small auxiliary network supplies continuous latent thoughts as input. Second, we produce those latents by recurrence: a tiny recurrent reasoner refines them over many steps, decoupling the depth of computation from the size of the model, so that the latents are a product of iterative processing rather than a single forward pass. We instantiate this as Latent Recurrent Thoughts (LRT): a task-dedicated proposer supplies base latents, a recurrent reasoner refines them through bounded residual corrections, and the frozen LLM decodes the answer. On symbolic reasoning with answer supervision but no reasoning traces (Countdown-4, Sudoku) and on natural-language reasoning (HumanEval, MBPP, StrategyQA), LRT substantially outperforms prior frozen-decoder continuous-space reasoning methods under an identical decoder, prompt, data, and training budget, and outperforms non-thinking-mode chain-of-thought prompting on the same backbone at a small fraction of its inference compute.

</details>


### [346] [Jailbreaking Text-to-Image Models Through Cracks: Navigating Heterogeneous Safety Filters via Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01168)
*Kaiyan Wen,Shijie Zhang,Lu Yu,Guangdong Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出检测面（Detection Surface）这一统一几何框架来描述文本到图像（T2I）生成模型中异构安全过滤器的决策边界，并基于此提出CRACK多智能体辩论框架，通过攻击、防御和裁判三个智能体的迭代协作实现自适应越狱搜索，在复合防御配置下达到高达99.63%的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I模型的越狱攻击研究要么针对单一过滤器优化，要么以聚合反馈查询完整安全管线，难以识别当前起作用的约束层并在跨层冲突中自适应调整攻击策略。

Method: 首先提出Detection Surface几何框架统一建模异构T2I安全过滤器的决策边界及其联合效果，揭示越狱成功的区域是稀疏且非凸的，由跨层冲突塑造。在此基础上提出CRACK框架，将越狱搜索分解为探索、诊断和仲裁三个环节，由攻击智能体、防御智能体和裁判智能体通过奖励引导的迭代辩论来生成提示突变、获取层特定诊断反馈并优化突变策略。

Result: 在多个T2I模型、数据集和安全配置上的广泛实验显示，CRACK在复合防御下攻击成功率最高达99.63%，所需查询次数少于现有方法，同时保持了语义保真度。

Conclusion: 通过检测面的几何洞察指导多智能体辩论式搜索，CRACK能够有效适应跨层安全约束的冲突，以更高的效率和成功率实现T2I模型的越狱攻击，揭示了当前复合防御体系的脆弱性。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks that elicit Not-Safe-For-Work (NSFW) content, despite increasingly being guarded by heterogeneous, multi-layer safety stacks combining text filters, image classifiers, and cross-modal detectors. Existing jailbreak studies either optimize against individual filters or query the complete pipeline with aggregate feedback, making it difficult to identify the active constraint and adapt to conflicts across safety layers.In this paper, we introduce the \emph{Detection Surface}, a unified geometric framework that characterizes the decision boundaries induced by heterogeneous T2I safety filters and their joint effect on the jailbreak search space. This formulation reveals that successful evasion is governed by a sparse and non-convex region shaped by cross-layer conflicts, where mutations that bypass one filter may increase exposure to another. Motivated by this analysis, we propose \emph{CRACK}, a multi-agent debate framework for adaptive jailbreak search that decomposes jailbreak search into exploration, diagnosis, and arbitration. CRACK coordinates an Attack Agent, a Defense Agent, and a Judge Agent to iteratively generate prompt mutations, obtain layer-specific diagnostic feedback, and optimize mutation strategies through reward-guided refinement. Through repeated rounds of debate, CRACK adapts its search direction to the evolving cross-layer constraints while preserving the original harmful intent. Extensive experiments across multiple T2I models, datasets, and safety configurations show that CRACK achieves Attack Success Rates (ASR) of up to 99.63\% under composite defenses, while requiring fewer queries than existing methods and maintaining semantic fidelity.

</details>


### [347] [FinLifeBench: Exhaustive Life-Event History and Financial-State Reconstruction from Longitudinal Banking Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01198)
*Hangyeul Lee,Juyoung Oh,Jaeyong Ko,Sunmin Kim,Jaeik Park,Hyunkyu Kim,Jungmin Son,Pilsung Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FinLifeBench基准测试，用于评估LLM在跨会话银行对话中长期维护客户生命周期记录的能力，包含6,000个韩语会话，覆盖24种事件类型和34条金融状态路径的确定性标注。实验发现11个LLM在长达300个会话的上下文中，事件回忆率从0.591降至0.445，金融状态重建的最佳GCA@15仅达0.470，且两个任务的表现相关性弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对话基准侧重于问答、有界片段或定向回忆，缺乏对跨会话日志式客户记录长期重建的评估，而真实银行场景中助手需要从日常请求中不断累积并维持完整、最新、可追溯的客户信息。

Method: 构建FinLifeBench基准，包含20条独立的合成轨迹、6,000个八轮韩语银行会话；定义两个任务——重建每个生活事件实例及其首次确立会话、在连续检查点重建完整的34路金融状态；采用确定性穷举标注（24种事件类型、34条状态路径）与共识质量保证；在完整上下文条件下评测11个LLM。

Result: 事件锚点回忆率从15个会话时的0.591下降到300个会话时的0.445；错误主要由事件遗漏而非锚点定位不准确导致；金融状态重建经常将过时或被取代的信息当作当前信息；最佳GCA@15仅达0.470；两个重建任务之间表现关联性弱。

Conclusion: 模型能够定位已恢复事件的证据，但仍无法在长期对话中维护完整且时间有效的纵向客户记录，揭示了当前LLM在长期记忆保持和时间有效性判断方面的显著不足。

Abstract: Repeated banking interactions require assistants to maintain complete, current, and traceable customer records as life changes emerge incidentally in routine requests. Existing benchmarks emphasize question answering, bounded episodes, or targeted recall rather than exhaustive longitudinal reconstruction. We introduce FinLifeBench, which evaluates two tasks over the same cumulative dialogue: reconstructing every life-event instance with its first-establishing session and reconstructing a complete 34-path financial state at consecutive checkpoints. The benchmark contains 6,000 eight-turn Korean banking sessions from 20 independent synthetic trajectories, with deterministic, exhaustive gold for 24 event types and 34 state paths and consensus quality assurance. Across eleven LLMs under a full-context condition, event-anchor recall falls from 0.591 at 15 sessions to 0.445 at 300. Errors are driven primarily by omitted events rather than poor anchor localization, while financial-state reconstruction frequently treats superseded or potentially outdated information as current; the best GCA@15 reaches 0.470. Performance on the two reconstruction tasks is only weakly associated. These results show that models can localize evidence for recovered events while still failing to maintain complete and temporally valid longitudinal records.

</details>


### [348] [H2Table: Hierarchical Hypergraph-Enhanced Large Language Models for Complex Table Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01216)
*Jia Ling,Yangfan Wang,Chen Tang,Haoming Tan,Yang Yang,Yi Guan,Jingchi Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出H2Table框架，将复杂表格表示为层次化嵌套超图，并通过定制的超图编码器和可学习查询向量将结构嵌入送入LLM，在HiTab数据集上对高复杂度表格（嵌套深度为四）相比最先进基线平均提升22.88%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将表格线性化为序列，忽略了表格固有的二维和层次化结构，导致LLM在复杂表格推理任务上表现不佳。

Method: 提出H2Table框架：(1) 将复杂表格表示为层次化嵌套超图；(2) 设计定制的超图编码器，通过超边（表头）与节点（单元格）之间的消息传递感知语义蕴含关系；(3) 引入一组可学习查询向量作为轻量桥梁，从编码器提取代表性结构嵌入传递给LLM。

Result: 实验表明该方法能有效处理具有层次化嵌套表头的复杂表格问答任务。在HiTab数据集的嵌套深度为四的高复杂表格上，H2Table相比最先进基线平均提升22.88%。

Conclusion: 层次化超图表示能够更好地捕捉复杂表格的结构信息，从而显著提升LLM在复杂表格推理任务上的性能。

Abstract: Tables are ubiquitous across diverse domains, yet reasoning over them remains a significant challenge for modern large language models (LLMs). Current approaches typically linearize tables into sequences, inherently overlooking their intrinsic two-dimensional and hierarchical structure. To address this, we propose H2Table (Hierarchical Hypergraph-Enhanced Table Reasoning), a novel framework that represents complex tables as hierarchical nested hypergraphs. To process this representation, we design a tailored hypergraph encoder to facilitate message passing between hyperedges (headers) and nodes (cells), thereby perceiving the semantic entailment relationships between them within complex tables. Furthermore, we introduce a set of learnable query vectors acting as a lightweight bridge to extract representative structural embeddings from the encoder into the LLM. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach effectively handles complex table question answering tasks with hierarchical nested headers. Notably, on the HiTab dataset, H2Table achieves an average improvement of 22.88% over state-of-the-art baselines on highly complex tables with a nesting depth of four. Our code is available at: https://github.com/lila120/h2table.

</details>


### [349] [Prompt-Robust Language Models: Which Training Strategies Work?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01217)
*Frederic Sadrieh,Michal Štefánik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了大型语言模型对提示（prompt）表述的敏感性，在受控条件下复现并比较了数据构建策略和鲁棒性微调方法。研究发现现有鲁棒微调方法虽优于标准微调和上下文学习，但最佳到最差提示的表现差距仍高达40-57%；且CoIN和PPCL等新兴鲁棒性增强方法往往不如最简单的数据构建策略（每batch使用单一模板）。诊断实验揭示了原因：辅助目标只能惩罚其所针对的量却无法泛化，且不同模板的梯度在57-64%的参数上方向冲突，导致混合式batch迫使优化器在竞争更新间妥协而非找到共享的、与提示无关的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽然性能强大，但对提示表述方式高度敏感，这严重影响了其在现实任务中的可靠性。现有研究通过改进数据构建或设计专门的鲁棒性训练目标来应对这一问题，但缺乏对这些策略在受控条件下的系统比较和有效性评估，因此需要深入了解不同方法的实际效果及其失败原因。

Method: 在受控条件下系统复现并比较了多种提示敏感性缓解策略，包括标准微调、上下文学习、数据构建策略（如每batch单模板训练），以及两种最新的鲁棒性增强方法CoIN（对比对齐）和PPCL（一致性正则化）。通过梯度分析诊断不同策略的效果差异，研究了不同模板间梯度的冲突情况。

Result: 鲁棒性微调方法确实优于标准微调和上下文学习，但最佳到最差提示的性能差距仍高達40-57%。CoIN和PPCL往往无法超越最简单且成本最低的数据构建策略（每batch训练一个模板）。辅助目标只能移动它们所惩罚的量，无法泛化到其他维度；此外，不同模板的梯度在57-64%的参数上符号相反，导致混合批次迫使优化器在冲突更新间妥协。

Conclusion: 现有鲁棒性增强方法在解决提示敏感性问题上的效果有限，简单的数据构建策略（单模板每batch训练）反而更为有效。研究表明，提高提示鲁棒性的关键在于避免优化器应对相互竞争的梯度更新，而应引导其找到在模板间共享的、与提示无关的参数解。

Abstract: Despite their strong performance, large language models remain highly sensitive to prompt formulation. Prior work addresses this through refined data construction or through dedicated robustness objectives. We reproduce and compare these strategies under controlled conditions, and measure how effective they are in addressing models' prompt sensitivity. We find the current robustness fine-tuning methods improve over standard fine-tuning and in-context learning, but the best-to-worst prompt gap remains as high as 40-57% of performance. Moreover, the recent robustness-enhancing methods we test - CoIN for contrastive alignment and PPCL for consistency regularization - often fail to outperform the simplest data construction strategy: training on one template per batch. Our diagnostics explain these results. The auxiliary objectives move the quantity they penalize, but do not generalize beyond it. Additionally, data construction strategies differ due to the conflicting signs of per-template gradients on 57-64% of parameters. Thus, batches that mix formulations force the optimizer to reconcile competing updates instead of finding a shared, prompt-agnostic one.

</details>


### [350] [Measuring the Behavioral Fidelity of Long-Horizon Human Activity Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01257)
*Yi Fei Cheng,Fan Yang,Iremsu Bas,Koichiro Niinuma,Narishige Abe,David Lindlbauer*

Main category: cs.AI
